	task.wait(0.07)

	img.Image = "rbxassetid://10456031512"
	task.wait(0.07)

	task.wait(0.07)

	img.Image = "rbxassetid://10456033399"
	task.wait(0.07)

	task.wait(0.07)

	img.Image = "rbxassetid://10456035806"

	task.wait(0.07)

	task.wait(0.07)

	img.Image = "rbxassetid://10456040874"

local function C_10()
local script = G2L["10"];
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		script.Parent.Parent.guis.Visible = false
		script.Parent.Parent.guis2.Visible = false
		script.Parent.Parent.main.Visible = false
		script.Parent.Parent.music.Visible = true
		script.Parent.Parent.music2.Visible = false
		script.Parent.Parent.guis.Visible = false
		script.Parent.Parent.main2.Visible = false
		script.Parent.Parent.scripts.Visible = false
		wait(0.1)
		script.Parent.Frame.Visible = true
	end)
end;
task.spawn(C_10);

-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.main.jump.LocalScript
local function C_18()
local script = G2L["18"];
	while true do
		script.Parent.TextColor3 = Color3.new(255/255,0/255,0/255)
		for i = 0,255,10 do
			wait()
			script.Parent.TextColor3 = Color3.new(255/255,i/255,0/255)
		end
		for i = 255,0,-10 do
			wait()
			script.Parent.TextColor3 = Color3.new(i/255,255/255,0/255)
		end
		for i = 0,255,10 do
			wait()
			script.Parent.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0/255,255/255,i/255)
		end
		for i = 255,0,-10 do
			wait()
			script.Parent.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0/255,i/255,255/255)
		end
		for i = 0,255,10 do
			wait()
			script.Parent.TextColor3 = Color3.new(i/255,0/255,255/255)
		end
		for i = 255,0,-10 do
			wait()
			script.Parent.TextColor3 = Color3.new(255/255,0/255,i/255)
		end
	end
end;
task.spawn(C_18);
-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.main.jump.LocalScript
local function C_19()
local script = G2L["19"];
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
		for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
			local pg = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
			if not pg then 
				continue 
			end
	
			-- ScreenGui untuk jumpscare
			local jumpscare = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
			jumpscare.ResetOnSpawn = false
			jumpscare.Parent = pg
	
			-- Gambar jumpscare
			local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
			img.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			img.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
			img.Image = "rbxassetid://97380420377987"
			img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			img.Parent = jumpscare
	
			-- Suara jumpscare
			local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://7405233417"
			sound.Looped = false
			sound.Volume = 10
			sound.Parent = jumpscare
			sound:Play()
	
			-- Warna layar berubah-ubah sampai 4 detik habis
			local running = true
	
			task.spawn(function()
				while running and img and img.Parent do
					task.wait()
					img.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(
						math.random(),
						math.random(),
						math.random()
					)
				end
			end)
	
			-- Tunggu 4 detik
			task.delay(4, function()
				running = false
				jumpscare:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end)
end;
task.spawn(C_19);

-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.main.sky.LocalScript
local function C_1d()
local script = G2L["1d"];
	function click()
		s = Instance.new("Sky")
		s.Name = "Sky"
		s.Parent = game.Lighting
		s.SkyboxBk = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=72557775719193"
		s.SkyboxDn = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=72557775719193"
		s.SkyboxFt = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=72557775719193"
		s.SkyboxLf = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=72557775719193"
		s.SkyboxRt = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=72557775719193"
		s.SkyboxUp = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=72557775719193"
		game.Lighting.TimeOfDay = 12
	end
	
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Down:connect(click)

-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.main.face.LocalScript
local function C_20()
local script = G2L["20"];
	function click()
		for i,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
				gui1=Instance.new("BillboardGui")
				gui1.Parent = v.Character.Head
				gui1.Adornee = v.Character.Head
				gui1.Size=UDim2.new(2.5,0,2.5,0)
				gui1.StudsOffset=Vector3.new(0,0.2,0)
				gui1.AlwaysOnTop = true
				text1=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
				text1.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=110648088268651"
				text1.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
				text1.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
				text1.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				text1.Parent=gui1
				v.Character.Head.Transparency = 1
			end
		end
	end
	
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Down:connect(click)
	
end;

-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.main.spam.LocalScript
local function C_23()
local script = G2L["23"];
	function click()
		local function exPro(root)
			for _, v in pairs(root:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Decal") and v.Texture ~= "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=118639017042041" then
					v.Parent = nil
				elseif v:IsA("BasePart") then
					v.Material = "Plastic"
					v.Transparency = 0
					local One = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					local Two = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					local Three = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					local Four = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					local Five = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					local Six = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					One.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=118639017042041"
					Two.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=118639017042041"
					Three.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=118639017042041"
					Four.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=118639017042041"
					Five.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=118639017042041"
					Six.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=118639017042041"
					One.Face = "Front"
					Two.Face = "Back"
					Three.Face = "Right"
					Four.Face = "Left"
					Five.Face = "Top"
					Six.Face = "Bottom"
				end
				exPro(v)
			end
		end
		local function asdf(root)
			for _, v in pairs(root:GetChildren()) do
				asdf(v)
			end
		end
		exPro(workspace)
		asdf(workspace)
	end
	
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Down:connect(click)
	
end;
task.spawn(C_23);

-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.main.hint.LocalScript
local function C_32()
local script = G2L["32"];
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
		local function write(text,object)
			if object:IsA("Hint") == true then
				local count = object:FindFirstChild("Count")
				if count == nil then
					count = Instance.new("NumberValue")
					count.Parent = object
					count.Name = "Count"
					count.Value = count.Value + 1
				else
					count.Value = count.Value + 1
				end
				local startcount = count.Value
				for i = 1,#text do
					if count.Value == startcount then
						local letter = string.sub(text,i,i)
						object.Text = string.sub(text,1,i)
						wait(0.001)
					else
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
		local bro = Instance.new("Hint",workspace)
		write("777ililo IS HERE TO FUCK THAT GAME!",bro)
	end)
	
	
end;
task.spawn(C_32);

-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.main.bbg.LocalScript
local function C_3c()
local script = G2L["3c"];
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		while true do
			-- Для каждого игрока в игре
			for _, player in pairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
					-- Создаём BillboardGui
					local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
					billboard.Parent = player.Character.Head -- прикрепляем к голове
					billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50) -- размеры BillboardGui
					billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0) -- сдвиг над головой
					billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true -- всегда поверх других объектов
	
					-- Создаём TextLabel для отображения текста
					local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
					textLabel.Parent = billboard
					textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
					textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1 -- без фона
					textLabel.Text = "777ililo"
					textLabel.TextScaled = true -- текст подстраивается по размеру
					textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	
	
					-- Меняем цвет текста
					local colors = {Color3.fromRGB(242, 255, 0), Color3.fromRGB(242, 255, 0), Color3.fromRGB(242, 255, 0)} -- красный, зелёный, синий
					for i = 1, #colors do
						textLabel.TextColor3 = colors[i]
						wait(98989898989989899) -- ждём 1 секунду перед сменой цвета
					end
	
					-- Удаляем BillboardGui после показа всех цветов
					billboard:Destroy()
				end
			end
	
			-- Добавляем паузу перед следующим циклом
			wait(1)
		end
	end)
end;
task.spawn(C_3c);

-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.main.DISCO.LocalScript
local function C_3f()
local script = G2L["3f"];
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
		local ColorCorrection = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect", Lighting)
	
		function zigzag(X) 
			return math.acos(math.cos(X*math.pi))/math.pi
		end
	
		Counter = 0
	
		Lighting.TimeOfDay = "15:00"
	
		while wait(0.01) do 
			ColorCorrection.TintColor = Color3.fromHSV(zigzag(Counter),1,1)
			Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromHSV(zigzag(Counter),1,1)
			Counter += 0.01
		end
	end)
end;
task.spawn(C_3f);

					s:Play()
					print("Screamed "..v.Name)

-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.main2.skvZ.LocalScript
local function C_61()
local script = G2L["61"];
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
		for _, player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
			local pg = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
			if not pg then 
				continue 
			end
	
			-- ScreenGui untuk jumpscare
			local jumpscare = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
			jumpscare.ResetOnSpawn = false
			jumpscare.Parent = pg
	
			-- Gambar jumpscare
			local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
			img.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			img.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
			img.Image = "rbxassetid://108052409439013"
			img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			img.Parent = jumpscare
	
			-- Suara jumpscare
			local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://7405233417"
			sound.Looped = false
			sound.Volume = 10
			sound.Parent = jumpscare
			sound:Play()
	
			-- Warna layar berubah-ubah sampai 4 detik habis
			local running = true
	
			task.spawn(function()
				while running and img and img.Parent do
					task.wait()
					img.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(
						math.random(),
						math.random(),
						math.random()
					)
				end
			end)
	
			-- Tunggu 4 detik
			task.delay(4, function()
				running = false
				jumpscare:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end)
end;
task.spawn(C_61);

-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.main2.rs.LocalScript
local function C_7d()
local script = G2L["7d"];
	function click()
		function exPro(root)
			for _, v in pairs(root:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Decal") and v.Texture ~= "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=78189084005932" then
					v.Parent = nil
				elseif v:IsA("BasePart") then
					v.Material = "Plastic"
					v.Transparency = 0
					local One = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					local Two = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					local Three = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					local Four = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					local Five = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					local Six = Instance.new("Decal", v)
					One.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=78189084005932"
					Two.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=78189084005932"
					Three.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=78189084005932"
					Four.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=78189084005932"
					Five.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=78189084005932"
					Six.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=78189084005932"
					One.Face = "Front"
					Two.Face = "Back"
					Three.Face = "Right"
					Four.Face = "Left"
					Five.Face = "Top"
					Six.Face = "Bottom"
				end
				exPro(v)
			end
		end
		function asdf(root)
			for _, v in pairs(root:GetChildren()) do
				asdf(v)
			end
		end
		exPro(workspace)
		asdf(workspace)
	end
	
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Down:connect(click)
	
	
	
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		for i, v in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetChildren()) do
			emit = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
			emit.Parent = v.Character.Head
			emit.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=78189084005932"
			emit.VelocitySpread = 100000
		end
	end)
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		for i, v in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetChildren()) do
			emit = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
			emit.Parent = v.Character.Head
			emit.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=78189084005932"
			emit.VelocitySpread = 100000
		end
	end)
	script.Parent.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

			emit = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
			emit.Parent = v.Character.Head

	end)

-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.main2.MLG.LocalScript
local function C_8e()
local script = G2L["8e"];
	local button = script.Parent
	
	button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		-- Pas player baru join
		game.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
			player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
				local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
				if torso then
					local pe = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", torso)
					pe.Texture = "rbxassetid://8979672"
					pe.VelocitySpread = 50
	
					local se = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", torso)
					se.Texture = "rbxassetid://176067516"
					se.VelocitySpread = 50
	
					local fe = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", torso)
					fe.Texture = "rbxassetid://179012130"
					fe.VelocitySpread = 50
				end
			end)
		end)
	
		-- Untuk player yang sudah ada
		for _, player in ipairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if player.Character then
				local torso = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or player.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
				if torso then
					local pe = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", torso)
					pe.Texture = "rbxassetid://8979672"
					pe.VelocitySpread = 50
	
					local se = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", torso)
					se.Texture = "rbxassetid://176067516"
					se.VelocitySpread = 50
	
					local fe = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", torso)
					fe.Texture = "rbxassetid://179012130"
					fe.VelocitySpread = 50
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end;

-- StarterGui.ScreenGui.Frame.UIDrag
local function C_d8()
local script = G2L["d8"];
	-- Made by Real_IceyDev (@lceyDex) --
	-- Simple UI dragger (PC Only/Any device that has a mouse) --
	
	local UIS = game:GetService('UserInputService')
	local frame = script.Parent
	local dragToggle = nil
	local dragSpeed = 0.25
	local dragStart = nil
	local startPos = nil
	
	local function updateInput(input)
		local delta = input.Position - dragStart
		local position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
			startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
		game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(dragSpeed), {Position = position}):Play()
	end
	
	frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then 
			dragToggle = true
			dragStart = input.Position
			startPos = frame.Position
			input.Changed:Connect(function()
				if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
					dragToggle = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	
	UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			if dragToggle then
				updateInput(input)
			end
		end
	end)
end;
task.spawn(C_d8);
